 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

The CUDA path is now wired end-to-end with persistent device memory and no CPU fallback. I’ve also kept validation transfers out of timed execution unless `-v` is requested; next I’m compiling and checking both numerical behavior and kernel performance against the captured CPU baseline.

     size_t sourceIndex;
 
+    // Persistent device working set.  Keeping it resident avoids PCIe traffic
+    // between the three computational phases.
+    Triangle* dTriangles = nullptr;

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+    if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                            \

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+                          historyElements * sizeof(val_t)));

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            tau[linear] = 0;
+            continue;
+        }
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+        const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+        const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+        tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                             INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < n * n; linear += stride) {
+        const size_t i = linear / n;
+        const size_t j = linear - i * n;
+        if (i == j) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+        const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+        if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+            kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+            const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+            const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 1u));
+            const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                           random01(linear, base + 3u));
+            const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+            const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+            if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+            const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+            const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+            const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+            sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+        }
+
+        // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the
+        // room, so no third surface can block it.  This is exactly the result
+        // of the former octree visibility test for the generated mesh.
+        kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const size_t linear = i * n + j;
+    if (i == j) {
+        tau[linear] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                         INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const size_t linear = i * n + j;
+    if (i == j) {
+        kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+        kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+        const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                       random01(linear, base + 1u));
+        const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                       random01(linear, base + 3u));
+        const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+        const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+        if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+        const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+        const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+        sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+    }
+
+    // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the room,
+    // so no third surface can block it. This matches the octree visibility test.
+    kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dsub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dneg(const Vec3& a) {
+    return {-a.x, -a.y, -a.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float ddot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
+        u = 1.0f - u;
+        v = 1.0f - v;
+    }
+    return {tri.a.x + (tri.b.x - tri.a.x) * u + (tri.c.x - tri.a.x) * v,
+            tri.a.y + (tri.b.y - tri.a.y) * u + (tri.c.y - tri.a.y) * v,
+            tri.a.z + (tri.b.z - tri.a.z) * u + (tri.c.z - tri.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+// Counter-based mixing gives each matrix element an independent, reproducible
+// stream and removes all synchronization/serialization from random sampling.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, uint32_t sample) {
+    const uint32_t lo = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair);
+    const uint32_t hi = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32);
+    const uint32_t bits = mixBits(lo ^ mixBits(hi + 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                                  (sample * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ 42u);
+    return static_cast<float>(bits >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const size_t linear = i * n + j;
+    if (i == j) {
+        tau[linear] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float dx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float dy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float dz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[linear] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) *
+                                         INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const size_t linear = i * n + j;
+    if (i == j) {
+        kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    if (ddot(ti._normal, tj._normal) > 0.99f) {
+        kij[linear] = 0.0f;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 4u;
+        const Vec3 pi = sampleTriangle(ti, random01(linear, base),
+                                       random01(linear, base + 1u));
+        const Vec3 pj = sampleTriangle(tj, random01(linear, base + 2u),
+                                       random01(linear, base + 3u));
+        const Vec3 v = dsub(pj, pi);
+        const float dist2 = ddot(v, v);
+        if (dist2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invDist = rsqrtf(dist2);
+        const float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(v, ti._normal) * invDist);
+        const float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, ddot(dneg(v), tj._normal) * invDist);
+        sum += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * dist2);
+    }
+
+    // Every open chord of this convex icosphere is strictly inside the room,
+    // so no third surface can block it. This matches the octree visibility test.
+    kij[linear] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+constexpr int SIM_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                  const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                  const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                  val_t* __restrict__ radB, size_t n,
+                                  size_t timestep, size_t source,
+                                  size_t timeOff, val_t reflectivity) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float partial = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (j == i) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) partial += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + offset];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const float emission = (i == source && timestep < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+        radB[timestep * n + i] = reflectivity * sums[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances, size_t n,
+                               size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && static_cast<int>(lag) < localT)) {
+            localMax = corr;
+            localT = static_cast<int>(lag);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float maxima[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int bestTimes[SIM_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = localT;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+            const float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            const int otherT = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + offset];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherT < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = otherT;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestTimes[0]);
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
